|                 <div>
|                   id="dynamic-content_LL_html-3-0"
|                   <h2>
|                     "What is a Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "A Railcard is a discount card that allows you to save up to 1/3 off the price of eligible train tickets, making it a great way to secure cheap fares."
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "What are the different types of UK Railcards?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "There are nine different types of UK Railcards available. These are:"
|                   "
"
|                   <ul>
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Network Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "16-17 Saver"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "16-25 Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "26-30 Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Family & Friends Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Two Together Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Senior Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Disabled Persons Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Veterans Railcard"
|                     "
"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "As an official retailer of Railcards from National Rail, you can buy seven of these in a digital format with us."
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Please note that the Disabled Persons Railcard and Veterans Railcard are "
|                     <strong>
|                       "not available to buy"
|                     " with us. We are currently working on the ability to verify eligibility."
|             <div>
|               class="base-container-item col-xs-12 col-sm-6 col-md-6"
|               <div>
|                 class="multi-column-container-wrapper"
|                 <div>
|                   id="dynamic-content_LL_html-3-1"
|                   <h2>
|                     "How much is a Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <ul>
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       <strong>
|                         "Price:"
|                       " Most Railcards cost just "
|                       <strong>
|                         "£35 for one year"
|                       " - or only £20 for the Disabled Persons Railcard."
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       <strong>
|                         "Savings:"
|                       " A Railcard gives you "
|                       <strong>
|                         "1/3 off rail fares"
|                       ", helping you travel for less."
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       <strong>
|                         "Best value:"
|                       " Choose a "
|                       <strong>
|                         "3-year Railcard for £80"
|                       " (equivalent to about £26 per year)."
|                     "
"
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "When can you use a Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Again, this depends on the type of Railcard you have. Generally, all Railcards can be used for discounted train fares during Off-Peak times, weekends and public holidays."
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Some, like the age-related Railcards, can be used during Peak times with a minimum fare, and the Disabled Persons Railcard can be used at any time."
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "Which tickets can you save money on?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "It varies depending on the type of Railcard you have, as some are valid for use during Peak times, but generally, as a Railcard holder you can save money on "

|                       href="https://www.thetrainline.com/trains/great-britain/ticket-types/advance-train-tickets"
|                       "Advance tickets"
|                     ","
|                     <a>
|                       href="https://www.thetrainline.com/trains/great-britain/ticket-types/off-peak-train-tickets"
|                       " Off-Peak"
|                     ", Standard, and "
|                     <a>
|                       href="https://www.thetrainline.com/trains/great-britain/ticket-types/first-class-train-tickets"
|                       "First Class tickets"
|                     "."

|                   <h2>
|                     "What is a digital Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Digital Railcards are stored within the Trainline app on your smartphone or tablet, meaning there's no need to carry the physical card around with you. They can be stored on any compatible device you travel with – there’s no limit on the number of devices you can have your digital Railcard on – and they can be used quicker than paper Railcards as they do not need to be sent in the post."
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "The minimum Operating System (OS) your Railcard would be available on is "
|                     <strong>
|                       "iOS 15.0 and above"
|                     " or "
|                     <strong>
|                       "Lollipop on Android"
|                     ". There are no device limits or download codes needed."
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "How do I use a digital Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Once you’ve bought a digital Railcard online with us, follow these easy steps to use it:"
|                   "
"
|                   <ol>
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "When searching for tickets on our website or in our app, tap ‘add a Railcard’ and scroll down to select the type of Railcard you’ve bought from the list"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "We'll automatically apply your Railcard savings and show you the discounted price"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Book your train tickets as a digital ticket, or to be collected at the station"
|                     "
"
|                     <li>
|                       "Bring the device your digital Railcard and train tickets are stored on to show to a ticket inspector if asked"
|                     "

|                     "How do I get a digital Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Digital Railcards can be bought online only – use our Railcard recommender at the top of the page to find the best type of Railcard that suits your needs. Once you have chosen your Railcard, simply tap the ‘Buy’ button and follow the instructions on the screen to get your digital Railcard."
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "How long does it take to receive a digital Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Your digital Railcard will automatically appear in your app straight after you have bought it. All you need to do is sign into your app to view your Railcard. "
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "Can I use my digital Railcard without internet?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "Yes. Your digital Railcard works offline. It's stored in the app, so you can show it to staff or validators even without a data connection or signal."
|                   "
"
|                   <h2>
|                     "What happens if my phone runs out of battery and I can't show my digital Railcard?"
|                   "
"
|                   <p>
|                     "You won’t be able to show your digital Railcard. Please make sure your phone is sufficiently charged before travelling so you can show your Railcard when needed. If you’re unable to show a valid Railcard, you may be asked to buy a single ticket or could be issued a Penalty Fare."

The Disabled Persons Railcard also has a three-year option but it’s cheaper and costs just £54."

They can be stored on any compatible device you travel with – there’s no limit on the number of devices you can have your digital Railcard on – and they can be used quicker than paper Railcards as they do not need to be sent in the post.</p>\n<p>The minimum Operating System (OS) your Railcard would be available on is <strong>iOS 15.0 and above</strong> or <strong>Lollipop on Android</strong>. There are no device limits or download codes needed.</p>\n<h2>How do I use a digital Railcard?</h2>\n<p>Once you’ve bought a digital Railcard online with us, follow these easy steps to use it:</p>\n<ol>\n<li>When searching for tickets on our website or in our app, tap ‘add a Railcard’ and scroll down to select the type of Railcard you’ve bought from the list</li>\n<li>We'll automatically apply your Railcard savings and show you the discounted price</li>\n<li>Book your train tickets as a digital ticket, or to be collected at the station</li>\n<li>Bring the device your digital Railcard and train tickets are stored on to show to a ticket inspector if asked</li>\n</ol>"}]},{"name":"html","properties":[{"key":"markup","value":"<h2>How do I get a digital Railcard?</h2>\n<p>Digital Railcards can be bought online only – use our Railcard recommender at the top of the page to find the best type of Railcard that suits your needs. Once you have chosen your Railcard, simply tap the ‘Buy’ button and follow the instructions on the screen to get your digital Railcard.</p>\n<h2>How long does it take to receive a digital Railcard?</h2>\n<p>Your digital Railcard will automatically appear in your app straight after you have bought it. All you need to do is sign into your app to view your Railcard. </p>\n<h2>Can I use my digital Railcard without internet?</h2>\n<p>Yes. Your digital Railcard works offline.
